<h2>v0.3.219</h2>
<h2>What's changed</h2>
<ul>
<li>Added opt-in <code>cancel_queued</code> to the interrupt control request (capability <code>interrupt_cancel_queued_v1</code>): cancels queued and pending-dispatch messages alongside the abort</li>
<li>Added <code>fast_mode_disabled_reason</code> to result and init messages so SDK hosts can explain why fast mode is off</li>
<li>Added <code>DirectoryAdded</code> lifecycle hook event to the control protocol, fired when a new working directory is registered mid-session</li>
<li>Fixed the initialize response reporting <code>fast_mode_state</code> from the spawn-time model after a model switch</li>
<li>Added <code>sandbox.network.strictAllowlist</code> to SDK settings types for deterministically denying non-allowlisted hosts in sandboxed commands</li>
<li>Added <code>workflowSizeGuideline</code> to SDK settings types for setting the advisory dynamic-workflow size guideline</li>
</ul>
<h2>Update</h2>
<pre lang="sh"><code>npm install @anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk@0.3.219
# or
yarn add @anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk@0.3.219
# or
pnpm add @anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk@0.3.219
# or
bun add @anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk@0.3.219
</code></pre>
<h2>v0.3.218</h2>
<h2>What's changed</h2>
<ul>
<li><code>SkillToolOutput</code> now reports <code>background: true</code> when a forked skill was dispatched as a detached background agent</li>
<li>Fixed the result event's <code>api_error_status</code> reporting null for rate-limit and overloaded errors delivered mid-stream; it now reports 429/529</li>
<li>Added <code>canonicalModel</code> and <code>provider</code> to each <code>modelUsage</code> entry in result messages so downstream billing can look up the correct rate table for <code>costUSD</code></li>
</ul>
<h2>Update</h2>
<pre lang="sh"><code>npm install @anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk@0.3.218

<details>
<summary>Changelog</summary>
<p><em>Sourced from <a href="https://github.com/anthropics/claude-agent-sdk-typescript/blob/main/CHANGELOG.md">@​anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk's changelog</a>.</em></p>
<blockquote>
<h2>0.3.220</h2>
<ul>
<li>Updated to parity with Claude Code v2.1.220</li>
</ul>
<h2>0.3.219</h2>
<ul>
<li>Added opt-in <code>cancel_queued</code> to the interrupt control request (capability <code>interrupt_cancel_queued_v1</code>): cancels queued and pending-dispatch messages alongside the abort</li>
<li>Added <code>fast_mode_disabled_reason</code> to result and init messages so SDK hosts can explain why fast mode is off</li>
<li>Added <code>DirectoryAdded</code> lifecycle hook event to the control protocol, fired when a new working directory is registered mid-session</li>
<li>Fixed the initialize response reporting <code>fast_mode_state</code> from the spawn-time model after a model switch</li>
<li>Added <code>sandbox.network.strictAllowlist</code> to SDK settings types for deterministically denying non-allowlisted hosts in sandboxed commands</li>
<li>Added <code>workflowSizeGuideline</code> to SDK settings types for setting the advisory dynamic-workflow size guideline</li>
</ul>
<h2>0.3.218</h2>
<ul>
<li><code>SkillToolOutput</code> now reports <code>background: true</code> when a forked skill was dispatched as a detached background agent</li>
<li>Fixed the result event's <code>api_error_status</code> reporting null for rate-limit and overloaded errors delivered mid-stream; it now reports 429/529</li>
<li>Added <code>canonicalModel</code> and <code>provider</code> to each <code>modelUsage</code> entry in result messages so downstream billing can look up the correct rate table for <code>costUSD</code></li>
</ul>
<h2>0.3.217</h2>
<ul>
<li>Changed subagents to no longer spawn nested subagents by default (depth cap lowered from 5 to 1); set <code>CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_SUBAGENT_SPAWN_DEPTH</code> to allow deeper nesting</li>
<li>Added a cap on concurrently-running subagents (default 20, override with <code>CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_CONCURRENT_SUBAGENTS</code>)</li>
<li>Fixed Remote Control sessions not re-sending pending permission prompts to clients that connect after the prompt appeared</li>
</ul>
<h2>0.3.216</h2>
<ul>
<li>Added optional <code>skippedLinks</code> count to <code>rewindFiles</code> responses for paths the rewind safety guards refused to restore or delete</li>
<li>Added <code>tool_result_meta</code> sidecar to user messages (<code>non_execution_kind</code>, <code>user_feedback</code>) so consumers can classify denied, interrupted, or cancelled tool calls without string-matching result prose</li>
<li>Added optional <code>user_message_uuid</code> and <code>request_sent_wall_ms</code> fields to the success result message for cross-host request-latency correlation</li>
</ul>
<h2>0.3.215</h2>
<ul>
<li>Updated to parity with Claude Code v2.1.215</li>
</ul>
<h2>0.3.214</h2>
<ul>
<li><code>set_permission_mode</code> now rejects unrecognized permission modes with an error instead of silently adopting them; the <code>'manual'</code> alias is accepted at every ingress</li>
<li>Added optional <code>subkind: 'scheduled-trigger'</code> to the <code>task-notification</code> member of <code>SDKMessageOrigin</code>, marking deliveries that are the fired prompt of a user-configured scheduled task</li>
<li><code>applyFlagSettings({effortLevel})</code> now accepts <code>'max'</code> in its TypeScript type (runtime already supported it)</li>
<li>Assistant messages truncated by <code>interrupt()</code> now carry <code>aborted: true</code>, so consumers can distinguish a mid-stream partial from a completed message</li>
<li>Added optional <code>subagent_type</code> and <code>subagent_retry</code> fields to <code>tool_progress</code> messages so clients can show a subagent waiting out an API rate-limit retry</li>
<li>The <code>system/init</code> message's <code>plugins</code> entries and the <code>reload_plugins</code> response now include each plugin's manifest <code>version</code></li>
<li>SessionStart hooks now report source <code>&quot;fork&quot;</code> instead of <code>&quot;resume&quot;</code> when the session begins as a fork</li>
</ul>
<h2>0.3.213</h2>
<ul>
<li>Updated to parity with Claude Code v2.1.213</li>
</ul>
<h2>0.3.212</h2>

<h2>pandas 3.0.5</h2>
<p>We are pleased to announce the release of pandas 3.0.5.
This is a patch release in the 3.0.x series and includes some regression fixes and bug fixes. We recommend that all users of the 3.0.x series upgrade to this version.</p>
<p>See the <a href="https://pandas.pydata.org/docs/whatsnew/v3.0.5.html">full whatsnew</a> for a list of all the changes.</p>
<p>Pandas 3.0 supports Python 3.11 and higher.
The release can be installed from PyPI:</p>
<pre><code>python -m pip install --upgrade pandas==3.0.*
</code></pre>
<p>Or from conda-forge</p>
<pre><code>conda install -c conda-forge pandas=3.0
</code></pre>
<p>Please report any issues with the release on the <a href="https://github.com/pandas-dev/pandas/issues">pandas issue tracker</a>.</p>
<p>Thanks to all the contributors who made this release possible.</p>
</blockquote>

</details>3e:T1210,# Overall Project Summary: Jul 29, 2026

The team successfully resolved a P0 production failure regarding agent deletion workflows and made significant strides in hardening CI/CD and calendar source-completeness. While core stability has improved, ongoing challenges remain with deployment race conditions and payout safety policies that require continued architectural focus.

## 🚨 Needs Attention 
- **Blocked Issues/PRs**: 
    - [#13100](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/issues/13100): Payout-rail branch is stalled due to safety gaps regarding Base-USDC launch policies and double-payment risks.
    - [#12080](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/issues/12080): Live showcase deployments are failing due to architectural defects; a proposal for org-scoped deployment records is currently under review.
- **Urgent Discussions**: 
    - [#17288](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/issues/17288): Requires input on the Aomi on-chain execution integration.
    - [#17268](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/issues/17268): Needs investigation into deterministic verification defects on Windows and UI.

## ✅ Completed Work

**Production Stability & Lifecycle Fixes**
*   Resolved a P0 production failure where agent deletion retries caused crashes due to timestamp serialization errors ([elizaos/eliza#17260](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/17260)).
*   Refactored deletion logic to prevent redundant timestamp writes during retry cycles ([elizaos/eliza#17251](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/17251)).
*   Implemented stricter Android component auditing to reject relocated privileged AAB components ([elizaos/eliza#17257](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/17257)).

**CI/CD and Infrastructure Hardening**
*   Repaired regressions in UI Story Gate and Windows CI environments ([elizaos/eliza#17264](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/17264), [elizaos/eliza#17266](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/17266)).
*   Fixed cloud-settings token scan failures on Windows caused by path separator inconsistencies ([elizaos/eliza#17259](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/17259)).

**Dependency Management & Tooling**
*   Performed a comprehensive dev-dependency sweep, including Biome updates and lockfile regeneration ([elizaos/eliza#17291](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/17291)).
*   Updated core dependencies including `uv` group, Capacitor plugins, and Anthropic SDKs ([elizaos/eliza#17183](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/17183), [elizaos/eliza#17281](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/17281), [elizaos/eliza#17280](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/17280), [elizaos/eliza#17279](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/17279)).
